```html
          Ningún vehículo requiere servicio en este momento.
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## Task 19: Prediction and clustering views (Unidades III and IV in the UI)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/analytics/forms.py`
- Modify: `apps/analytics/views.py`, `apps/analytics/urls.py`, `apps/analytics/tests.py`
- Create: `templates/analytics/predictions.html`, `templates/analytics/clusters.html`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ml.supervised.predict_delay`, `ml.evaluation.load_metrics`, `ml.unsupervised.CLUSTER_PATH`.
- Produces:
  - `apps.analytics.forms.DelayPredictionForm` — a `forms.Form` whose `to_features() -> dict` returns a mapping over `datasets.FEATURE_COLUMNS`.
  - Views `predictions`, `clusters`; URL names `analytics_predictions`, `analytics_clusters`.

```python
class PredictionViewTest(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        call_command("loaddata", "delay_causes", verbosity=0)
        call_command("seed_demo", months=4, seed=42, verbosity=0)
        call_command("run_etl", rebuild=True, verbosity=0)
        call_command("train_models", verbosity=0)

    def test_prediction_form_renders_with_model_metrics(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("analytics_predictions"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Matriz de confusión")
        self.assertIn("metrics", response.context)

    def test_posting_a_delivery_returns_a_prediction(self):
        from apps.routes.models import Route

        route = Route.objects.first()
        response = self.client.post(reverse("analytics_predictions"), {
            "route": route.pk,
            "departure_hour": 8,
            "day_of_week": 1,
            "cargo_weight_kg": "5000",
            "packages_count": "40",
            "vehicle_type": "TRUCK",
            "vehicle_age_range": "9+",
            "operator_seniority_range": "0-2",
            "customer_type": "PREMIUM",
        })
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        prediction = response.context["prediction"]
        self.assertIn("will_be_late", prediction)
        self.assertIn("probability", prediction)

    def test_clusters_view_shows_named_groups(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("analytics_clusters"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("summary", response.context)
        self.assertGreater(len(response.context["summary"]), 1)
        self.assertContains(response, "Rutas")


class UntrainedModelTest(TestCase):
    def test_prediction_view_explains_how_to_train(self):
        from ml import supervised, unsupervised

        for path in (
            supervised.CLASSIFIER_PATH, supervised.REGRESSOR_PATH,
            unsupervised.CLUSTER_PATH,
        ):
            if path.exists():
                path.unlink()

        response = self.client.get(reverse("analytics_predictions"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "train_models")

    def test_clusters_view_explains_how_to_train(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("analytics_clusters"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "train_models")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `apps/analytics/forms.py`**

```python
"""Capture form for a prospective delivery.

The form asks only for facts that exist before departure — the same contract
the classifier was trained under. It derives the remaining feature columns from
the chosen route so the user cannot introduce an inconsistency.
"""

from django import forms

from apps.routes.models import Route
from warehouse.models import TIME_BANDS

DAY_CHOICES = [
    (0, "Lunes"), (1, "Martes"), (2, "Miércoles"), (3, "Jueves"),
    (4, "Viernes"), (5, "Sábado"), (6, "Domingo"),
]
VEHICLE_TYPE_CHOICES = [
    ("TRUCK", "Camión"), ("VAN", "Camioneta"),
    ("TRAILER", "Tráiler"), ("PICKUP", "Pick-up"),
]
AGE_CHOICES = [("0-3", "0 a 3 años"), ("4-8", "4 a 8 años"), ("9+", "9 años o más")]
SENIORITY_CHOICES = [
    ("0-2", "0 a 2 años"), ("3-5", "3 a 5 años"), ("6+", "6 años o más"),
]
CUSTOMER_TYPE_CHOICES = [
    ("PREMIUM", "Premium"), ("REGULAR", "Regular"), ("OCCASIONAL", "Ocasional"),
]


def _distance_range(km):
    if km < 80:
        return "CORTA"
    if km < 350:
        return "MEDIA"
    return "LARGA"


class DelayPredictionForm(forms.Form):
    route = forms.ModelChoiceField(
        label="Ruta", queryset=Route.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("code"),
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    departure_hour = forms.IntegerField(
        label="Hora de salida", min_value=0, max_value=23, initial=8,
        widget=forms.NumberInput(attrs={"class": "form-control"}),
    )
    day_of_week = forms.TypedChoiceField(
        label="Día de la semana", choices=DAY_CHOICES, coerce=int, initial=1,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    cargo_weight_kg = forms.DecimalField(
        label="Peso de carga (kg)", min_value=1, initial=5000,
        widget=forms.NumberInput(attrs={"class": "form-control", "step": "0.01"}),
    )
    packages_count = forms.IntegerField(
        label="Bultos", min_value=1, initial=40,
        widget=forms.NumberInput(attrs={"class": "form-control"}),
    )
    vehicle_type = forms.ChoiceField(
        label="Tipo de vehículo", choices=VEHICLE_TYPE_CHOICES,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    vehicle_age_range = forms.ChoiceField(
        label="Antigüedad del vehículo", choices=AGE_CHOICES,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    operator_seniority_range = forms.ChoiceField(
        label="Antigüedad del operador", choices=SENIORITY_CHOICES,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    customer_type = forms.ChoiceField(
        label="Tipo de cliente", choices=CUSTOMER_TYPE_CHOICES,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )

    def to_features(self):
        """Build the exact feature mapping the pipeline expects."""
        data = self.cleaned_data
        route = data["route"]
        hour = data["departure_hour"]
        day = data["day_of_week"]
        return {
            "distance_km": float(route.distance_km),
            "planned_duration_min": int(route.estimated_duration_min),
            "cargo_weight_kg": float(data["cargo_weight_kg"]),
            "packages_count": int(data["packages_count"]),
            "day_of_week": day,
            "route_code": route.code,
            "route_type": route.route_type,
            "zone": route.zone,
            "distance_range": _distance_range(float(route.distance_km)),
            "time_band": TIME_BANDS[hour],
            "vehicle_type": data["vehicle_type"],
            "vehicle_age_range": data["vehicle_age_range"],
            "operator_seniority_range": data["operator_seniority_range"],
            "customer_type": data["customer_type"],
            "is_weekend": "True" if day >= 5 else "False",
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Append the two views to `apps/analytics/views.py`**

```python
import joblib

from apps.analytics.forms import DelayPredictionForm
from ml import evaluation, supervised, unsupervised


def predictions(request):
    """Unidad III in the UI: score a delivery and show how the model performs."""
    form = DelayPredictionForm(request.POST or None)
    prediction, error = None, None

    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        try:
            prediction = supervised.predict_delay(form.to_features())
        except FileNotFoundError as failure:
            error = str(failure)

    metrics = evaluation.load_metrics()
    if not metrics:
        error = error or (
            "Los modelos no están entrenados. Ejecuta "
            "'python manage.py train_models' para generarlos."
        )

    return render(request, "analytics/predictions.html", {
        "section": "predictions",
        "warehouse_empty": False,
        "form": form,
        "prediction": prediction,
        "error": error,
        "metrics": metrics.get("classification"),
        "regression": metrics.get("regression"),
    })


def clusters(request):
    """Unidad IV in the UI: the PCA plane and the profile of every group."""
    if not unsupervised.CLUSTER_PATH.exists():
        return render(request, "analytics/clusters.html", {
            "section": "clusters",
            "warehouse_empty": False,
            "error": (
                "El modelo de agrupamiento no está entrenado. Ejecuta "
                "'python manage.py train_models' para generarlo."
            ),
        })

    artifact = joblib.load(unsupervised.CLUSTER_PATH)
    metrics = evaluation.load_metrics().get("clustering", {})
    components = artifact["components"]
    summary = artifact["profile_summary"].reset_index()

    scatter = {}
    for label, group in components.groupby("label"):
        scatter[label] = [
            {"x": round(float(x), 3), "y": round(float(y), 3), "route": index}
            for index, x, y in zip(group.index, group["pc1"], group["pc2"])
        ]

    return render(request, "analytics/clusters.html", {
        "section": "clusters",
        "warehouse_empty": False,
        "error": None,
        "metrics": metrics,
        "summary": summary.to_dict("records"),
        "scatter_json": scatter,
        "explained": [
            round(value * 100, 1) for value in metrics.get("explained_variance", [])
        ],
    })
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `templates/analytics/predictions.html`**

```html
{% extends "analytics/base_analytics.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Predicción de retraso{% endblock %}

{% block analytics %}
{% if error %}<div class="alert alert-warning"><code>{{ error }}</code></div>{% endif %}

<div class="row g-3">
  <div class="col-lg-5">
    <div class="card shadow-sm h-100">
      <div class="card-header">¿Llegará tarde esta entrega?</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <form method="post" novalidate>
          {% csrf_token %}
          <div class="row g-3">
            {% for field in form %}
            <div class="col-md-6">
              <label class="form-label" for="{{ field.id_for_label }}">{{ field.label }}</label>
              {{ field }}
              {% for e in field.errors %}<div class="text-danger small">{{ e }}</div>{% endfor %}
            </div>
            {% endfor %}
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-primary mt-3" type="submit">Calcular predicción</button>
        </form>

        {% if prediction %}
        <hr>
        <div class="alert {% if prediction.will_be_late %}alert-danger{% else %}alert-success{% endif %} mb-2">
          <div class="fs-5 fw-semibold">
            {% if prediction.will_be_late %}Probable retraso{% else %}Llegada a tiempo{% endif %}
          </div>
          Probabilidad de retraso: <strong>{{ prediction.probability|floatformat:3 }}</strong>
        </div>
        <p class="mb-0">
          Retraso esperado:
          <strong>{{ prediction.expected_delay_minutes|floatformat:1 }} minutos</strong>
          <span class="text-muted small">(regresión lineal)</span>
        </p>
        {% endif %}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="col-lg-7">
    <div class="card shadow-sm h-100">
      <div class="card-header">Evaluación del modelo</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        {% if metrics %}
        <p class="mb-2">
          Algoritmo seleccionado: <strong>{{ metrics.best_name }}</strong>
          <span class="text-muted small">
            — entrenamiento {{ metrics.train_size }} filas, prueba {{ metrics.test_size }} filas
          </span>
        </p>
        <div class="table-responsive mb-3">
          <table class="table table-sm">
            <thead class="table-light"><tr>
              <th>Algoritmo</th><th class="text-end">Exactitud</th>
              <th class="text-end">Precisión</th><th class="text-end">Sensibilidad</th>
              <th class="text-end">F1</th><th class="text-end">ROC-AUC</th>
              <th class="text-end">F1 (VC 5)</th>
            </tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {% for candidate in metrics.candidates %}
              <tr {% if candidate.name == metrics.best_name %}class="table-success"{% endif %}>
                <td>{{ candidate.name }}</td>
                <td class="text-end">{{ candidate.metrics.accuracy|floatformat:3 }}</td>
                <td class="text-end">{{ candidate.metrics.precision|floatformat:3 }}</td>
                <td class="text-end">{{ candidate.metrics.recall|floatformat:3 }}</td>
                <td class="text-end fw-semibold">{{ candidate.metrics.f1|floatformat:3 }}</td>
                <td class="text-end">{{ candidate.metrics.roc_auc|floatformat:3 }}</td>
                <td class="text-end">{{ candidate.cv_f1_mean|floatformat:3 }}</td>
              </tr>
              {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {% if regression %}
        <p class="mb-2">
          Regresión lineal —
          MSE <strong>{{ regression.metrics.mse|floatformat:2 }}</strong> ·
          RMSE {{ regression.metrics.rmse|floatformat:2 }} ·
          MAE <strong>{{ regression.metrics.mae|floatformat:2 }}</strong> ·
          R² {{ regression.metrics.r2|floatformat:3 }}
        </p>
        {% endif %}

        <div class="row g-3">
          <div class="col-md-6">
            <img src="{% static 'ml/confusion_matrix.png' %}" class="img-fluid"
                 alt="Matriz de confusión">
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-6">
            <img src="{% static 'ml/residuals.png' %}" class="img-fluid"
                 alt="Residuales de la regresión">
          </div>
        </div>

        <h6 class="mt-4">Variables más influyentes</h6>
        <ol class="small mb-0">
          {% for name, weight in metrics.feature_importances|slice:":10" %}
          <li>{{ name }} <span class="text-muted">({{ weight|floatformat:4 }})</span></li>
          {% endfor %}
        </ol>
        {% else %}
        <p class="text-muted mb-0">Sin métricas disponibles.</p>
        {% endif %}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

```html
{% extends "analytics/base_analytics.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Conglomerados de rutas{% endblock %}

{% block analytics %}
{% if error %}
<div class="alert alert-warning"><code>{{ error }}</code></div>
{% else %}

<div class="row g-3 mb-3">
  <div class="col-6 col-lg-3">
    <div class="card kpi-card shadow-sm"><div class="card-body">
      <div class="kpi-label text-muted">Conglomerados</div>
      <div class="kpi-value">{{ metrics.k }}</div>
    </div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="col-6 col-lg-3">
    <div class="card kpi-card shadow-sm"><div class="card-body">
      <div class="kpi-label text-muted">Coeficiente de silueta</div>
      <div class="kpi-value">{{ metrics.silhouette|floatformat:3 }}</div>
    </div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="col-6 col-lg-3">
    <div class="card kpi-card shadow-sm"><div class="card-body">
      <div class="kpi-label text-muted">Davies-Bouldin</div>
      <div class="kpi-value">{{ metrics.davies_bouldin|floatformat:3 }}</div>
    </div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="col-6 col-lg-3">
    <div class="card kpi-card shadow-sm"><div class="card-body">
      <div class="kpi-label text-muted">Varianza explicada</div>
      <div class="kpi-value">
        {% for value in explained %}{{ value }}%{% if not forloop.last %} + {% endif %}{% endfor %}
      </div>
    </div></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="row g-3">
  <div class="col-lg-7">
    <div class="card shadow-sm h-100">
      <div class="card-header">Rutas en el plano de componentes principales</div>
      <div class="card-body"><canvas id="pcaChart" height="260"></canvas></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="col-lg-5">
    <div class="card shadow-sm h-100">
      <div class="card-header">Diagnóstico del agrupamiento</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <img src="{% static 'ml/elbow.png' %}" class="img-fluid mb-3" alt="Método del codo">
        <img src="{% static 'ml/silhouette.png' %}" class="img-fluid" alt="Silueta por k">
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="card shadow-sm mt-3">
  <div class="card-header">Perfil de cada conglomerado</div>
  <div class="table-responsive">
    <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
      <thead class="table-light"><tr>
        <th>Conglomerado</th><th class="text-end">Rutas</th>
        <th class="text-end">Distancia media</th><th class="text-end">Duración media</th>
        <th class="text-end">Tasa de retraso</th><th class="text-end">Retraso medio</th>
        <th class="text-end">Envíos/mes</th><th class="text-end">$/km</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        {% for row in summary %}
        <tr>
          <td class="fw-semibold">{{ row.label }}</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.routes }}</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.distance_km|floatformat:0 }} km</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.avg_duration_min|floatformat:0 }} min</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.delay_rate|floatformat:2 }}</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.avg_delay_minutes|floatformat:1 }} min</td>
          <td class="text-end">{{ row.monthly_shipments|floatformat:1 }}</td>
          <td class="text-end">${{ row.avg_cost_per_km|floatformat:2 }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>
</div>
{% endif %}
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
{% if not error %}
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.3/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
{{ scatter_json|json_script:"scatter-data" }}
<script>
  const palette = ["#0d6efd", "#dc3545", "#198754", "#fd7e14", "#6f42c1",
                   "#20c997", "#d63384", "#6c757d", "#ffc107", "#0dcaf0"];
  const scatter = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("scatter-data").textContent);
  new Chart(document.getElementById("pcaChart"), {
    type: "scatter",
    data: {
      datasets: Object.entries(scatter).map(([label, points], index) => ({
        label: label,
        data: points,
        backgroundColor: palette[index % palette.length],
        pointRadius: 5
      }))
    },
    options: {
      plugins: {
        legend: { position: "bottom" },
        tooltip: {
          callbacks: {
            label: context => `${context.dataset.label} — ${context.raw.route}`
          }
        }
      },
      scales: {
        x: { title: { display: true, text: "Componente principal 1" } },
        y: { title: { display: true, text: "Componente principal 2" } }
      }
    }
  });
</script>
{% endif %}
{% endblock %}
```

**Files:**

```python
class ExportTest(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        call_command("loaddata", "delay_causes", verbosity=0)
        call_command("seed_demo", months=3, seed=42, verbosity=0)
        call_command("run_etl", rebuild=True, verbosity=0)

    def test_csv_export_returns_a_downloadable_file(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("analytics_export", args=["rutas", "csv"])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response["Content-Type"], "text/csv; charset=utf-8")
        self.assertIn("attachment", response["Content-Disposition"])
        self.assertIn("rutas", response["Content-Disposition"])

    def test_csv_export_has_a_header_row_in_spanish(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("analytics_export", args=["rutas", "csv"])
        )
        first_line = response.content.decode("utf-8-sig").splitlines()[0]
        self.assertIn("Código", first_line)
        self.assertIn("Envíos", first_line)

    def test_excel_export_returns_a_workbook(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("analytics_export", args=["costos-vehiculo", "xlsx"])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            response["Content-Type"],
            "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
        )
        self.assertTrue(response.content.startswith(b"PK"))

    def test_every_declared_report_exports(self):
        from apps.analytics import exports

        for slug in exports.REPORTS:
            response = self.client.get(reverse("analytics_export", args=[slug, "csv"]))
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, slug)

    def test_unknown_report_returns_404(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("analytics_export", args=["inexistente", "csv"])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `apps/analytics/exports.py`**

```python
"""Tabular exports of the analytical reports.

Each report declares its Spanish column headers next to the key that produces
them, so CSV and Excel stay in step with the on-screen tables.
"""

import csv
from io import BytesIO

import pandas as pd
from django.http import Http404, HttpResponse

from apps.analytics import queries

REPORTS = {
    "rutas": {
        "label": "Rutas más utilizadas",
        "builder": lambda: queries.top_routes(limit=100),
        "columns": [
            ("code", "Código"), ("name", "Ruta"), ("zone", "Zona"),
            ("shipments", "Envíos"), ("delay_rate", "% retraso"),
        ],
    },
    "rutas-retrasadas": {
        "label": "Rutas con mayores retrasos",
        "builder": lambda: queries.worst_routes(limit=100),
        "columns": [
            ("code", "Código"), ("name", "Ruta"), ("zone", "Zona"),
            ("shipments", "Envíos"), ("avg_delay", "Retraso promedio (min)"),
            ("delay_rate", "% retraso"),
        ],
    },
    "operadores": {
        "label": "Operadores con más entregas",
        "builder": lambda: queries.top_operators(limit=100),
        "columns": [
            ("employee_number", "Empleado"), ("full_name", "Nombre"),
            ("deliveries", "Entregas"), ("delay_rate", "% retraso"),
        ],
    },
    "costos-vehiculo": {
        "label": "Costo total por vehículo",
        "builder": lambda: queries.cost_by_vehicle(limit=200),
        "columns": [
            ("economic_number", "Económico"), ("plate", "Placa"),
            ("vehicle_type", "Tipo"), ("age_range", "Antigüedad"),
            ("fuel_cost", "Combustible"), ("maintenance_cost", "Mantenimiento"),
            ("total_cost", "Total"),
        ],
    },
    "rendimiento": {
        "label": "Rendimiento por vehículo",
        "builder": lambda: queries.efficiency_by_vehicle(limit=200),
        "columns": [
            ("economic_number", "Económico"), ("vehicle_type", "Tipo"),
            ("age_range", "Antigüedad"), ("efficiency", "km/L"),
            ("liters", "Litros"),
        ],
    },
    "costo-por-km": {
        "label": "Costo por kilómetro por ruta",
        "builder": lambda: queries.cost_per_km_by_route(limit=200),
        "columns": [
            ("code", "Código"), ("name", "Ruta"),
            ("cost_per_km", "Costo por km"), ("shipments", "Envíos"),
        ],
    },
}


def _report_or_404(slug):
    if slug not in REPORTS:
        raise Http404(f"Reporte desconocido: {slug}")
    return REPORTS[slug]


def to_csv(slug):
    report = _report_or_404(slug)
    response = HttpResponse(content_type="text/csv; charset=utf-8")
    response["Content-Disposition"] = f'attachment; filename="{slug}.csv"'
    response.write("﻿")            # BOM so Excel reads the accents
    writer = csv.writer(response)
    writer.writerow([header for _, header in report["columns"]])
    for row in report["builder"]():
        writer.writerow([row[key] for key, _ in report["columns"]])
    return response


def to_excel(slug):
    report = _report_or_404(slug)
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            {header: row[key] for key, header in report["columns"]}
            for row in report["builder"]()
        ]
    )
    buffer = BytesIO()
    with pd.ExcelWriter(buffer, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        frame.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name=report["label"][:31])
    buffer.seek(0)

    response = HttpResponse(
        buffer.read(),
        content_type=(
            "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
        ),
    )
    response["Content-Disposition"] = f'attachment; filename="{slug}.xlsx"'
    return response
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Append the view and the URL**

In `apps/analytics/views.py`:

```python
from apps.analytics import exports


def export_report(request, slug, fmt):
    """Serve a report as CSV or Excel. The format is the only branch here."""
    if fmt == "xlsx":
        return exports.to_excel(slug)
    return exports.to_csv(slug)
```

In `apps/analytics/urls.py`:

```python
urlpatterns += [
    path(
        "exportar/<slug:slug>.<str:fmt>",
        views.export_report,
        name="analytics_export",
    ),
]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `docs/Manual_Usuario.md`**

Required sections, one chapter per module, each with its screenshot:

1. **Introducción** — what SIG-LOG is, who uses it, what it answers.
2. **Cómo ingresar** — starting the server, the address, the navigation bar.
3. **Capítulo por módulo** (Clientes, Vehículos, Operadores, Rutas, Entregas, Combustible, Mantenimiento). Each chapter carries: what the module is for · how to search · how to register (field by field, with the validation rules from the form) · how to edit · what "dar de baja" means (the record is kept for auditing) · the screenshot.
4. **Registrar la llegada de una entrega** — the arrival flow, and the rule that a delay over 15 minutes requires a cause.
5. **Reportes y análisis** — one section per analytics screen, explaining **how to read each chart**, not just where to click. For the Pareto: "the bars are counts, the line is the running percentage; the causes to the left of 80% are where to act first." For the heatmap: "darker means more departures; the dark columns are your saturated hours."
6. **Predicción de retraso** — how to fill the form, and what the probability means. State plainly that the model estimates likelihood, not certainty.
7. **Conglomerados de rutas** — how to read the PCA plane and the profile table; what each cluster name implies operationally.
8. **Exportar reportes** — the CSV and Excel buttons.
9. **Preguntas frecuentes** — at minimum: empty dashboard (run the ETL), untrained model (run `train_models`), a vehicle not appearing (it was deactivated), why a delivery cannot be deleted (`PROTECT` on the foreign keys).

4. **Estructura del código** — the directory tree, and what each package owns.
5. **El motor CRUD genérico** — how to add a ninth module in forty lines, with a complete worked example.
6. **El proceso ETL** — the three phases, the two extraction types, the full table of cleaning rules, and how to read `dw.etl_log` and `dw.etl_error`. Include the two verification queries from Task 12 Step 7.
7. **Los modelos de minería** — the pipelines, the leakage exclusion and why it matters, how to retrain, where artifacts live.

9. **Pruebas** — how to run them, what each suite covers.
10. **Mantenimiento y solución de problemas** — at minimum: migrations failing because the schema is missing (run `0001_create_schemas`), the ETL rejecting everything (check `dw.etl_error.rule`), the classifier below threshold (spec §14 mitigation), a port already in use.
11. **Despliegue** — `DEBUG=False`, `collectstatic`, `ALLOWED_HOSTS`, and a note that a production deployment needs a WSGI server, which is outside this project's scope.

The unit asks for four specific things; give each its own section:

1. **Esquema del data warehouse** — the star diagram, the seven dimensions with their attributes, the three facts with their grain and measures, and the justification for star over snowflake and for three facts over one.
2. **Tipos y fuentes de datos** — the eight source tables, classified as structured / semi-structured / unstructured (all structured here — say so and say why), the volume of each, and the two extraction types with when to use each.
3. **Técnicas de limpieza de datos** — the full table from spec §5.4, and for each rule: what it detects, what it does, and **the real count of rows it rejected** from the `dw.etl_error` query in Step 1.
4. **Parámetros de configuración** — schemas, batch size (2000), SCD type, `EFFICIENCY_BOUNDS`, `DELAY_TOLERANCE_MINUTES`, the connection settings, and the `run_etl` flags.

Close with the `dw.etl_log` table pasted as evidence of a successful run.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `docs/U3_Analisis_Supervisado.md`**

1. **Justificación del algoritmo utilizado** — why two classifiers were compared rather than one asserted; what logistic regression buys (interpretability) and what random forest buys (interactions); the F1 criterion for choosing; and why linear regression is the right model for the minutes question.
2. **Descripción del diseño del modelo** — the feature table, the pipeline (`StandardScaler` + `OneHotEncoder` inside `Pipeline`), the 80/20 stratified split, the 5-fold cross-validation, and a dedicated subsection **"Exclusión de variables por fuga de datos"** listing the excluded columns and explaining that including them would yield near-perfect and worthless accuracy.
3. **Reporte de evaluación y optimización** — the real metrics table for both candidates (accuracy, precision, recall, F1, ROC-AUC, cross-validated F1), the confusion matrix figure, the regression metrics (**MSE, RMSE, MAE, R²**), the residual plot, and the top fifteen feature importances.
4. **Interpretación** — read the results back to the business: which factors actually drive delay, and whether the model recovered the patterns that were seeded. If any threshold adjustment from Task 14 Step 4 was applied, record it here.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `docs/U4_Analisis_No_Supervisado.md`**

1. **Justificación del algoritmo** — why PCA (decorrelates distance and duration, makes the result drawable) and why K-means (interpretable centroids, works on a small route set); what was rejected (DBSCAN, hierarchical) and why.
2. **Descripción de los resultados** — the k sweep table from 2 to 10 with inertia, silhouette and Davies-Bouldin; the elbow and silhouette figures; the chosen k with its justification; the explained variance of both components; the PCA scatter figure; and the profile table with each cluster's name.
3. **Reporte de evaluación y optimización** — the three metrics for the chosen k against the 0.40 threshold, and what the sweep says about neighbouring values of k.
4. **Interpretación operativa** — for each cluster, what it means and what the company should do about it.

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `docs/U5_Visualizacion.md`**

1. **Técnicas de visualización empleadas** — for each chart type used (line, stacked bar, horizontal bar, doughnut, Pareto, heatmap, scatter), say what it is good at and why it was chosen for that particular question.
2. **Herramientas y bibliotecas** — Chart.js for the interactive dashboard, matplotlib for model diagnostics; the division of labour and its rationale; a comparison note against Excel and Power BI, which the syllabus names.
3. **Código fuente de las gráficas** — the syllabus requires the plotting code in the repository. Reference `ml/evaluation.py` for matplotlib and the `{% block scripts %}` sections of the analytics templates for Chart.js, and paste one complete example of each.
4. **Dashboard y gráficas personalizadas** — every screenshot from `docs/img/`, each with its reading.
5. **Interpretación de resultados** — the closing section: answer all ten questions from spec §1 with the real numbers, one paragraph each, and state what the company should do about each answer.

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit**

```powershell
git add -A
git commit -m "docs: add one analysis document per thematic unit"
```

## Task 23: End-to-end verification from a clean database

The last task exists because "it works on my machine after twenty commands" is not the same as the spec's success criterion 1.

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/Verificacion_Entrega.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Drop and recreate the database**

```powershell
& "C:\Program Files\PostgreSQL\18\bin\dropdb.exe" -U postgres -h localhost --if-exists siglog
& "C:\Program Files\PostgreSQL\18\bin\createdb.exe" -U postgres -h localhost siglog
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the documented startup sequence exactly as the README states it**

```powershell
python manage.py migrate
python manage.py loaddata delay_causes
python manage.py seed_demo --months 18
python manage.py run_etl --rebuild
python manage.py train_models
```

Every command must succeed with no manual intervention. If any step needs a fix the README does not mention, **the README is wrong** — correct it, not the procedure.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the full test suite**

```powershell
python manage.py test -v 2
```

Expected: `OK`. Record the total count.

- [ ] **Step 4: Check each success criterion from spec §2.2 and write down the result**

```powershell
python manage.py shell -c "from warehouse import models as dw; print('dims', [m.objects.count() for m in (dw.DimDate, dw.DimTime, dw.DimCustomer, dw.DimVehicle, dw.DimOperator, dw.DimRoute, dw.DimDelayCause)]); print('facts', dw.FactDelivery.objects.count(), dw.FactFuel.objects.count(), dw.FactMaintenance.objects.count())"
python manage.py shell -c "from ml.evaluation import load_metrics; m = load_metrics(); print('F1', m['classification']['best']['metrics']['f1']); print('silueta', m['clustering']['silhouette'])"
```

Criterion 4 requires F1 ≥ 0.75; criterion 5 requires silhouette ≥ 0.40.


Visit all fourteen URLs and confirm each renders without error:
`/` · `/clientes/` · `/vehiculos/` · `/operadores/` · `/rutas/` · `/entregas/` · `/combustible/` · `/mantenimiento/` · `/reportes/` · `/reportes/operacion/` · `/reportes/costos/` · `/reportes/mantenimiento/` · `/reportes/prediccion/` · `/reportes/conglomerados/`

Then register one delivery arrival end to end, and download one CSV and one Excel export.

## Self-review notes

**Spec coverage.** Every section of the spec maps to a task:

| Spec | Task |
|---|---|
| §3.3 structure, §3.4 CRUD engine | 1, 2 |
| §4.1–4.4 catalogues | 3, 4 |
| §4.5–4.6 deliveries, §4.9 arrival service | 5 |
| §4.7 fuel, §4.9 efficiency | 6 |
| §4.8 maintenance | 7 |
| §6 generator | 8 |
| §5.1–5.3 schemas, dimensions, facts, §5.5 logs | 9 |
| §5.4 extract | 10 |
| §5.4 transform and cleaning | 11 |
| §5.4 load, §5.6 CLI | 12 |
| §7.1 feature contract | 13 |
| §7.1 supervised models | 14 |
| §7.2 unsupervised models | 15 |
| §7.4 training command, figures | 16 |
| §8 dashboard | 17, 18, 19, 20 |
| §11 documentation | 21, 22 |
| §2.2 success criteria | 23 |
| §9 error handling | folded into 2, 5, 7, 12, 17, 19 |
| §10 testing | folded into every task |
| §7.3 optional customer clustering | deliberately not scheduled — spec marks it out of scope |

**Two deviations from the spec, both stated in the tasks that make them:** `next_service_km` and `last_service_date` are denormalized onto `Vehicle` (Task 4), and `route_code` joins the feature set with congestion seeded by zone (Global Constraints, Task 13).

**Type consistency.** `EtlRun.phase()` yields a counter with `read` / `written` / `rejected` in Tasks 10–12; `transform.run` returns the eight keys that `load.run` reads; `datasets.FEATURE_COLUMNS` is the same list consumed by `supervised.build_preprocessor`, `predict_delay` and `DelayPredictionForm.to_features`; `cluster_routes` returns the keys the `train_models` command and the `clusters` view both read.
